# s015 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. P1 15:1-15 | Inscribed and Guarded Transmission
- Semantic invariant: A message is assembled into an ordered form, delivered, and retained in human custody.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:1 (ءَايَٰتُ, كِتَٰبِ, قُرْءَانٍ, مُّبِينٍ), 15:6 (ذِّكْرُ, نُزِّلَ), 15:9 (نَزَّلْ, ذِّكْرَ, حَٰفِظُونَ), 15:10-12 (أَرْسَلْ, رَّسُولٍ, نَسْلُكُ, قُلُوبِ).
- Surprising reach: The lexical field materializes revelation as joined writing, a registered decree, retained memory, and thread inserted through a heart.

#### Subchannel A. Assembled Readable Artifact
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Marks are joined into writing, gathered as recitation, and made semantically visible.
- Active motifs: visible mark (`quranic:root_000074:B003/m01`); joined components (`quranic:root_001283:B001/m01`); lettered writing (`quranic:root_001283:B002/m01`); binding decree (`quranic:root_001283:B003/m01`); gathered whole (`quranic:root_001210:B001/m01`); reading and recitation (`quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`); disclosure by speech or sign (`quranic:root_000170:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:1 (ءَايَٰتُ, كِتَٰبِ, قُرْءَانٍ, مُّبِينٍ); 15:4 (كِتَابٌ).
- Synthesis: The opening text is not only something read. Its marks are joined, gathered, and fixed with decretive force, while disclosure makes that assembled form intelligible.

#### Subchannel B. Delivered, Retained, and Threaded
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A dispatched message is brought down, held in memory, and inserted through the receptive interior.
- Active motifs: delivery to a destination (`quranic:root_001492:B002/m01`); recollection retained after absence (`quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`); utterance on the tongue (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`); guarding custody (`quranic:root_000342:B001/m01`); retention in the self (`quranic:root_000342:B002/m01`); messenger and message (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`); insertion into another thing (`quranic:root_000735:B002/m01`); sewing thread (`quranic:root_000735:B004/m01`); heart as interior center (`quranic:root_001248:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:6 (ذِّكْرُ, نُزِّلَ); 15:8-9 (نُنَزِّلُ, نَزَّلْ, ذِّكْرَ, حَٰفِظُونَ); 15:10-12 (أَرْسَلْ, رَّسُولٍ, نَسْلُكُ, قُلُوبِ).
- Synthesis: Sending and guarding form one transmission circuit. The thread sense of *slk* gives the heart-passage a concrete operation: the message is sewn into an interior where it can be retained or resisted.

### 2. P1 15:1-15 | Appointed Limit and Ordered Succession
- Semantic invariant: Collective existence proceeds inside a fixed temporal and documentary bound.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:4 (قَرْيَةٍ, كِتَابٌ, مَّعْلُومٌ), 15:5 (أُمَّةٍ, أَجَلَ, تَسْبِقُ, يَسْتَـْٔخِرُ), 15:8 (مُّنظَرِينَ).
- Surprising reach: Time behaves like both a registered allotment and a race whose participants cannot overtake or delay the assigned line.

#### Subchannel A. Settlement Entered in the Ledger
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A gathered settlement is assigned a known entry, period, and terminal bound.
- Active motifs: gathered settlement (`quranic:root_001222:B001/m01`); binding decree (`quranic:root_001283:B003/m01`); entry in a register (`quranic:root_001283:B004/m01`); disclosed knowledge (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); terminal duration (`quranic:root_000016:B001/m01`); community as a period of time (`quranic:root_000053:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:4 (قَرْيَةٍ, كِتَابٌ, مَّعْلُومٌ); 15:5 (أُمَّةٍ, أَجَلَ).
- Synthesis: The settlement is treated as a gathered unit whose life is entered in a ledger. Its destruction is therefore framed as the completion of an already bounded temporal entry.

#### Subchannel B. Neither Overtaking nor Deferring
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A community advances toward an endpoint but cannot cross it early or hold it back.
- Active motifs: competitive precedence (`quranic:root_000671:B001/m01`); postponement (`quranic:root_000019:B002/m01`); terminal duration (`quranic:root_000016:B001/m01`); community or kind (`quranic:root_000053:B004/m01`); community as time-span (`quranic:root_000053:B008/m01`); granted delay (`quranic:root_001520:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:5 (أُمَّةٍ, أَجَلَ, تَسْبِقُ, يَسْتَـْٔخِرُ); 15:8 (مُّنظَرِينَ).
- Synthesis: The temporal order is simultaneously a course and a deferment regime. Neither speed nor requested respite changes the endpoint assigned to the collective.

### 3. P1 15:1-15 | Reception Converted into Distortion
- Semantic invariant: Evidence reaches its audience, but hostile reception recodes it as derangement or disables the faculty that would perceive it.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:6 (قَالُ, مَجْنُونٌ), 15:10-11 (أَرْسَلْ, رَّسُولٍ, يَسْتَهْزِءُ), 15:14-15 (بَابًا, سَّمَآءِ, يَعْرُجُ, أَبْصَٰرُ, سُكِّرَتْ, مَّسْحُورُونَ).
- Surprising reach: Ridicule and impaired sight become parallel closure operations, one social and one perceptual.

#### Subchannel A. Messenger Speech Recast as Derangement
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Delivered speech is answered by mockery and a charge that the speaker's mind is covered.
- Active motifs: spoken utterance (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); messenger and message (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`); ridicule (`quranic:root_001587:B001/m01`); covering of reason by madness (`quranic:root_000266:B006/m01`); delivery to a destination (`quranic:root_001492:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:6 (قَالُ, مَجْنُونٌ, نُزِّلَ); 15:8-11 (نُنَزِّلُ, نَزَّلْ, أَرْسَلْ, رَّسُولٍ, يَسْتَهْزِءُ); 15:15 (قَالُ).
- Synthesis: The audience does not leave the message unclassified; it converts transmission into a pathology of the transmitter. Mockery thus functions as a social screen over delivered speech.

#### Subchannel B. Opened Sky and Disabled Sight
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A gate opens and ascent occurs, yet vision is stilled, intoxicated, and explained away as magic.
- Active motifs: entrance gate (`quranic:root_000163:B001/m01`); opening of a closed passage (`quranic:root_001124:B001/m01`); overshadowing sky (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`); upward ascent (`quranic:root_000997:B004/m01`); eyesight (`quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`); intoxicated loss of clarity (`quranic:root_000725:B001/m01`); stilling after motion (`quranic:root_000725:B004/m01`); magical deflection and deception (`quranic:root_000682:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:14 (بَابًا, فَتَحْ, سَّمَآءِ, يَعْرُجُ); 15:15 (أَبْصَٰرُ, سُكِّرَتْ, مَّسْحُورُونَ).
- Synthesis: Even maximal access does not guarantee reception. The scene opens the passage while closing interpretation, rendering direct ascent as intoxicated vision and deceptive redirection.

### 4. P1 15:1-15 | Appetite and Deferred Response
- Semantic invariant: Intake and enjoyment occupy the body and attention while response is postponed.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:3 (يَأْكُلُ, يَتَمَتَّعُ, يُلْهِ, أَمَلُ), with latent extensions anchored at 15:4 and 15:6-9.
- Surprising reach: A brief command about eating expands into a full feeding circuit, while hope behaves like an extended grant of time.

#### Subchannel A. Feeding Circuit from Provision to Throat
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Food is assembled, lodged in a vessel, fed into an opening, and taken through the mouth and throat.
- Active motifs: eating (`quranic:root_000043:B001/m01`); edible provision (`quranic:root_000043:B003/m01`); food vessel (`quranic:root_000043:B013/m01`); gathered food for a guest (`quranic:root_001222:B003/m01`); provision prepared for an arrival (`quranic:root_001492:B005/m01`); feeding the mill-mouth (`quranic:root_001382:B003/m01`); uvula at the throat (`quranic:root_001382:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:3 (يَأْكُلُ, يُلْهِ); 15:4 (قَرْيَةٍ); 15:6, 15:8-9 (نُزِّلَ, نُنَزِّلُ, نَزَّلْ).
- Synthesis: The latent senses give eating a concrete route through provision, container, opening, and throat. This material circuit sharpens the surface image of bodily occupation.

#### Subchannel B. Pleasure Stretches Hope
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Enjoyment extends attention into anticipated time and distracts it from the approaching limit.
- Active motifs: pleasurable benefit (`quranic:root_001395:B001/m01`); prolonged enjoyment (`quranic:root_001395:B007/m01`); distraction by another thing (`quranic:root_001382:B001/m01`); hope with waiting (`quranic:root_000052:B001/m01`); fixed, deliberate looking (`quranic:root_000052:B002/m01`); postponement (`quranic:root_000019:B002/m01`); granted delay (`quranic:root_001520:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:3 (يَتَمَتَّعُ, يُلْهِ, أَمَلُ); 15:5 (يَسْتَـْٔخِرُ); 15:8 (مُّنظَرِينَ).
- Synthesis: Pleasure does more than distract in the present; it lengthens the horizon of expectation. Hope, gaze, and deferment form one attentional mechanism that makes a fixed limit feel remote.

### 5. P2 15:16-25 | Fortified Heaven and Pursued Intrusion
- Semantic invariant: A visible, ornamented height is organized as guarded space whose boundary reacts to covert entry.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:16 (بُرُوجًا, زَيَّ, سَّمَآءِ, نَّٰظِرِينَ), 15:17-18 (حَفِظْ, شَيْطَٰنٍ, رَّجِيمٍ, ٱسْتَرَقَ, سَّمْعَ, أَتْبَعَ, شِهَابٌ).
- Surprising reach: Constellations become watchtowers, observation becomes sentry duty, and the pursuing flame takes the role of a combatant.

#### Subchannel A. Ornamented Watch-Fort
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Celestial towers make an elevated enclosure both beautiful and observable while sentries preserve its boundary.
- Active motifs: fortified towers (`quranic:root_000101:B001/m01`); celestial towers (`quranic:root_000101:B002/m01`); applied adornment (`quranic:root_000660:B002/m01`); overshadowing sky (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`); directed observation (`quranic:root_001520:B001/m01`); observing guard (`quranic:root_001520:B009/m01`); protective custody (`quranic:root_000342:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:16 (بُرُوجًا, زَيَّ, سَّمَآءِ, نَّٰظِرِينَ); 15:17 (حَفِظْ).
- Synthesis: Beauty and defense occupy the same architecture. What appears to the observer as ornament also functions as a system of towers, watch, and custody.

#### Subchannel B. Covert Listener under Pursuit
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A distant rebel steals a hearing, is detected, pursued, and driven off by a flame-warrior.
- Active motifs: rebellious adversary (`quranic:root_000796:B004/m01`); distance and severance (`quranic:root_000796:B001/m01`); covert listening (`quranic:root_000700:B002/m01`); auditory reception (`quranic:root_000741:B001/m01`); catching pursuit (`quranic:root_000175:B002/m01`); radiant flame (`quranic:root_000821:B002/m01`); combatant advancing in war (`quranic:root_000821:B003/m01`); defense of the group (`quranic:root_000547:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:17 (شَيْطَٰنٍ, رَّجِيمٍ); 15:18 (ٱسْتَرَقَ, سَّمْعَ, أَتْبَعَ, شِهَابٌ).
- Synthesis: The eavesdropping episode forms a complete border encounter: remote approach, covert acquisition, detection, chase, and repulsion. The flame is not merely an object but an active defender.

### 6. P2 15:16-25 | Stored Provision and Measured Release
- Semantic invariant: Provision moves from guarded reserve to recipients through controlled dispatch.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:21 (خَزَآئِنُ, نُنَزِّلُ, قَدَرٍ, مَّعْلُومٍ), 15:22 (أَرْسَلْ, رِّيَٰحَ, لَوَٰقِحَ, أَنزَلْ, مَآءً, أَسْقَيْ, خَٰزِنِينَ).
- Surprising reach: The weather sequence behaves like a managed supply system with storehouse, keeper, metering, carrier, and delivery.

#### Subchannel A. Treasury, Keeper, and Meter
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A reserve is guarded, measured through deliberate planning, and delivered in an assigned share.
- Active motifs: secured storage (`quranic:root_000406:B001/m01`); keeper of the store (`quranic:root_000406:B003/m01`); delivery to a destination (`quranic:root_001492:B002/m01`); bounded quantity (`quranic:root_001205:B001/m01`); planned apportionment (`quranic:root_001205:B005/m01`); disclosed knowledge (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); allotted irrigation share and channel (`quranic:root_000722:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:21 (خَزَآئِنُ, نُنَزِّلُ, قَدَرٍ, مَّعْلُومٍ); 15:22 (أَسْقَيْ, خَٰزِنِينَ, أَنزَلْ).
- Synthesis: The treasury image becomes operational: a keeper preserves stock, measure determines quantity, and a channel assigns its destination. Descent is therefore a controlled transfer rather than undifferentiated abundance.

#### Subchannel B. Wind-Carried Irrigation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Wind is dispatched to fertilize, water descends and becomes an administered drink.
- Active motifs: dispatch (`quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`); wind and moving air (`quranic:root_000609:B003/m01`); impregnation and fertilization (`quranic:root_001368:B001/m01`); overshadowing sky (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`); water as substance (`quranic:root_001458:B001/m01`); conveyance by water (`quranic:root_001458:B003/m01`); giving drink (`quranic:root_000722:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:22 (أَرْسَلْ, رِّيَٰحَ, لَوَٰقِحَ, سَّمَآءِ, مَآءً, أَسْقَيْ).
- Synthesis: The wind is a carrier with a reproductive function, and rain is the cargo it helps place. Irrigation completes the dispatch by transferring that cargo into drinkable provision.

### 7. P2 15:16-25 | Generative Relay
- Semantic invariant: Life propagates through fertilization, emergence, nourishment, and continued growth.
- Surface relation: indirect; 15:19 (أَرْضَ, أَنۢبَتْ), 15:22 (رِّيَٰحَ, لَوَٰقِحَ, مَآءً, أَسْقَيْ), 15:23 (نُحْىِۦ), 15:25 (رَبَّ).
- Surprising reach: The fertilizing wind opens into both plant development and animal conception, milk, and drink.

#### Subchannel A. Soil to Sprout
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Fertile ground receives generative force, produces a plant, and sustains its unfolding life.
- Active motifs: fertile soil (`quranic:root_000025:B002/m01`); plant emerging from earth (`quranic:root_001465:B001/m01`); deliberate planting (`quranic:root_001465:B002/m01`); plant under nurture (`quranic:root_000532:B012/m01`); earth revived by rain (`quranic:root_000383:B002/m01`); tree bursting into growth (`quranic:root_000609:B017/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:19 (أَرْضَ, أَنۢبَتْ); 15:22 (رِّيَٰحَ); 15:23 (نُحْىِۦ); 15:25 (رَبَّ).
- Synthesis: Growth appears as a relay from receptive ground to emergence and nurture. Rain-life and wind-driven bursting make the measured vegetation a continuing process rather than a static yield.

#### Subchannel B. Conception, Milk, and Drink
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Fertilization produces gestation and lactation, whose flowing milk becomes nourishment.
- Active motifs: conception (`quranic:root_001368:B001/m01`); milk-bearing she-camel (`quranic:root_001368:B002/m01`); seminal water in the womb (`quranic:root_001458:B004/m01`); successive milk flow (`quranic:root_000563:B006/m01`); giving drink (`quranic:root_000722:B001/m01`); camel-water mixed with feed (`quranic:root_001407:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:19 (مَدَدْ); 15:22 (أَرْسَلْ, لَوَٰقِحَ, مَآءً, أَسْقَيْ).
- Synthesis: The same roots that surface as fertilizing winds and water also assemble an animal reproductive economy. Conception, gestation, milk flow, and drinking materialize provision as intergenerational nourishment.

### 8. P2 15:16-25 | Stable Expanse and Enabled Passage
- Semantic invariant: Stability is produced by extension, weight, and anchoring so that a traversable world can hold.
- Surface relation: indirect; 15:16 (بُرُوجًا), 15:19 (أَرْضَ, مَدَدْ, أَلْقَيْ, رَوَٰسِىَ, مَّوْزُونٍ), 15:22 (أَرْسَلْ, رِّيَٰحَ, مَآءً).
- Surprising reach: Terrestrial anchoring is mirrored by a ship, mooring, water, and favorable wind.

#### Subchannel A. Extended and Weighted Ground
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Lower ground is stretched, weighted, balanced, and fixed by cast anchors.
- Active motifs: lower land opposite the sky (`quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`); longitudinal extension (`quranic:root_001407:B001/m01`); stable emplacement (`quranic:root_000564:B001/m01`); casting into place (`quranic:root_001372:B005/m01`); measured weight (`quranic:root_001645:B001/m01`); balancing correspondence (`quranic:root_001645:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:19 (أَرْضَ, مَدَدْ, أَلْقَيْ, رَوَٰسِىَ, مَّوْزُونٍ).
- Synthesis: The earth scene is an engineering operation: extension creates span, casting places the stabilizers, and weight supplies balance. The result is a held expanse rather than inert terrain.

#### Subchannel B. Ship under Wind and Mooring
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A vessel crosses abundant water under wind, then secures itself at an anchorage.
- Active motifs: ship (`quranic:root_000101:B007/m01`); ship at anchor (`quranic:root_000564:B002/m01`); easy travel (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); propelling wind (`quranic:root_000609:B003/m01`); abundant visible water (`quranic:root_001458:B002/m01`); long tension rope (`quranic:root_000796:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:16 (بُرُوجًا); 15:19 (رَوَٰسِىَ); 15:22 (أَرْسَلْ, رِّيَٰحَ, مَآءً).
- Synthesis: The same anchoring logic that stabilizes the earth generates a maritime scene. Wind enables movement, while rope and mooring convert motion back into stable placement.

### 9. P2 15:16-25 | Succession Drawn into Assembly
- Semantic invariant: Life passes from one holder to another before all positions are collected under one authority.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:23 (نُحْىِۦ, نُمِيتُ, وَٰرِثُونَ), 15:24 (مُسْتَقْدِمِينَ, مُسْتَـْٔخِرِينَ, عَلِمْ), 15:25 (يَحْشُرُ, رَبَّ, عَلِيمٌ).
- Surprising reach: Inheritance and temporal ordering become stages in one transfer-and-collection mechanism.

#### Subchannel A. Life Transferred through Death
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Living force departs one bearer and what remains passes to a successor.
- Active motifs: living creature (`quranic:root_000383:B003/m01`); loss of force and life (`quranic:root_001454:B001/m01`); inheritance transferred from predecessor (`quranic:root_001639:B001/m01`); uncontracted succession (`quranic:root_001639:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:23 (نُحْىِۦ, نُمِيتُ, وَٰرِثُونَ).
- Synthesis: Life, death, and inheritance form a transfer sequence. The heir does not interrupt mortality but receives what the prior bearer can no longer hold.

#### Subchannel B. Fore and Aft Driven to One Destination
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Earlier and later positions are known, then the separated ranks are driven into one assembly.
- Active motifs: forward precedence (`quranic:root_001207:B004/m01`); later position (`quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`); driving a group to a destination (`quranic:root_000324:B001/m01`); disclosed knowledge (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); sovereign authority (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:24 (مُسْتَقْدِمِينَ, مُسْتَـْٔخِرِينَ, عَلِمْ); 15:25 (يَحْشُرُ, رَبَّ, عَلِيمٌ).
- Synthesis: Sequence does not disperse the generations indefinitely. Knowledge holds every position, and sovereign gathering converts fore and aft into a single destination.

### 10. P3 15:26-44 | Formation through Charged Media
- Semantic invariant: Distinct beings are formed when measured matter receives an activating medium.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:26-29 (حَمَإٍ, صَلْصَٰلٍ, خَلَقْ, بَشَرًا, سَوَّيْ, نَفَخْ, رُّوحِ) and 15:27 (سَّمُومِ, نَّارِ, جَآنَّ).
- Surprising reach: Dry clay is acoustically resonant, while breath and hot wind are opposed modes of penetrating animation.

#### Subchannel A. Measured Resonant Body
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Black mud is measured, dried into sounding clay, straightened into form, and given a skin-defined human surface.
- Active motifs: black mud in water (`quranic:root_000354:B001/m01`); dry sounding clay (`quranic:root_000878:B002/m01`); repeated resonance in dry matter (`quranic:root_000878:B001/m01`); measured formation (`quranic:root_000434:B001/m01`); created production (`quranic:root_000434:B002/m01`); integral straightened form (`quranic:root_000766:B002/m01`); visible skin surface (`quranic:root_000120:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:26, 15:28, 15:33 (حَمَإٍ, صَلْصَٰلٍ, خَلَقْ, بَشَرًا); 15:29 (سَوَّيْ).
- Synthesis: Creation is rendered as a craft sequence from wet dark material to dry resonant form. The sounding quality makes the body a shaped cavity or shell ready for activation.

#### Subchannel B. Vital Breath and Penetrating Fire-Wind
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Air enters and animates one form, while a hot penetrating current constitutes the prior hidden being.
- Active motifs: air blown into a thing (`quranic:root_001529:B001/m01`); inflation by air (`quranic:root_001529:B002/m01`); vital spirit (`quranic:root_000609:B001/m01`); narrow aperture of entry (`quranic:root_000743:B001/m01`); penetrating hot wind (`quranic:root_000743:B004/m01`); kindled fire (`quranic:root_001564:B002/m01`); hidden being (`quranic:root_000266:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:27 (جَآنَّ, سَّمُومِ, نَّارِ); 15:29 (نَفَخْ, رُّوحِ).
- Synthesis: Breath, spirit, aperture, and inflation assemble an animation mechanism. The hot wind and fire scene mirrors that mechanism with a harsher medium, placing the two creations in material contrast.

### 11. P3 15:26-44 | Posture Determines Placement
- Semantic invariant: Bodily orientation discloses allegiance and determines whether a participant remains within the ordered assembly.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:29-33 (قَعُ, سَٰجِدِينَ, سَجَدَ, أَجْمَعُونَ, كُلُّ, أَبَىٰٓ), 15:34-35 (ٱخْرُجْ, رَجِيمٌ, لَّعْنَةَ).
- Surprising reach: Prostration is a coordinated fall into order, while pride is an inflation that ends in expulsion.

#### Subchannel A. Assembly Falls into Alignment
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A complete host lowers itself together, bowing and falling into a unified posture.
- Active motifs: abasement and lowering (`quranic:root_000675:B001/m01`); bowed head (`quranic:root_000675:B004/m01`); falling and becoming fixed (`quranic:root_001675:B001/m01`); gathering separated members (`quranic:root_000259:B001/m01`); complete totality (`quranic:root_001315:B003/m01`); cohesive strength (`quranic:root_001444:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:28-30 (مَلَٰٓئِكَةِ, قَعُ, سَٰجِدِينَ, سَجَدَ, أَجْمَعُونَ, كُلُّ); 15:31-33 (سَّٰجِدِينَ, أَسْجُدَ).
- Synthesis: The host does not merely obey individually. Falling, bowing, gathering, and cohesion make prostration a collective alignment of strength under command.

#### Subchannel B. Inflated Refusal and Expulsion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Refusal swells into pride, breaks participation, and is answered by exit, banishment, and exclusion.
- Active motifs: severe refusal (`quranic:root_000008:B001/m01`); swelling pride (`quranic:root_001529:B006/m01`); passage outside (`quranic:root_000400:B001/m01`); expulsion and banishment (`quranic:root_000547:B003/m01`); exclusion under wrath (`quranic:root_001359:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:31 (أَبَىٰٓ); 15:34 (ٱخْرُجْ, رَجِيمٌ); 15:35 (لَّعْنَةَ).
- Synthesis: Pride is figured as bodily inflation, the inverse of lowered prostration. That expansion does not enlarge status; it ruptures membership and pushes the refuser outside.

### 12. P3 15:26-44 | Governed Line and Adorned Deviation
- Semantic invariant: Movement is organized either by a straight, protected line of service or by an attractive deviation that ends in capture.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:39-42 (زَيِّنَ, أَغْوَيْ, عِبَادَ, مُخْلَصِينَ, صِرَٰطٌ, مُسْتَقِيمٌ, سُلْطَٰنٌ, ٱتَّبَعَ, غَاوِينَ).
- Surprising reach: Misguidance behaves like a hunter's pit, while the straight path is reinforced by both authority and exclusive belonging.

#### Subchannel A. Straight Route under Authority
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A straight road is held by governing force, demonstrative authority, service, and exclusive attachment.
- Active motifs: straight road (`quranic:root_000858:B001/m01`); rectitude and balance (`quranic:root_001273:B008/m01`); coercive power (`quranic:root_000732:B001/m01`); compelling proof (`quranic:root_000732:B002/m01`); obedient service (`quranic:root_000973:B003/m01`); exclusive belonging (`quranic:root_000430:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:40 (مُخْلَصِينَ, عِبَادَ); 15:41 (صِرَٰطٌ, مُسْتَقِيمٌ); 15:42 (سُلْطَٰنٌ, عِبَادِ).
- Synthesis: Straightness is not only geometry. It is a governed relation in which proof, power, service, and exclusive attachment keep movement on the protected line.

#### Subchannel B. Decorated Track into a Pit
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An attractive surface induces trailing movement that becomes deviation, liability, and entrapment.
- Active motifs: applied beautification (`quranic:root_000660:B002/m01`); deviation in dark falsehood (`quranic:root_001116:B001/m01`); hunter's killing pit (`quranic:root_001116:B004/m01`); following behind (`quranic:root_000175:B001/m01`); attached liability (`quranic:root_000175:B006/m01`); stalled movement (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:39 (زَيِّنَ, أَغْوَيْ); 15:42 (ٱتَّبَعَ, غَاوِينَ).
- Synthesis: Adornment supplies the lure, following supplies the motion, and the pit supplies the latent outcome. The scene pressures a purely cognitive account of error by making deviation a guided capture.

### 13. P3 15:26-44 | Appointed Judgment and Partitioned Entry
- Semantic invariant: Judgment is temporally fixed and spatially apportioned.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:35-38 (دِّينِ, يَوْمِ, أَنظِرْ, مُنظَرِينَ, وَقْتِ, مَعْلُومِ), 15:43-44 (مَوْعِدُ, أَجْمَعِينَ, أَبْوَٰبٍ, سَبْعَةُ, جُزْءٌ, مَّقْسُومٌ).
- Surprising reach: A deadline becomes architecture: the scheduled collective reaches doors that distribute it into assigned portions.

#### Subchannel A. Fixed Respite
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Requested delay is granted only inside a marked duration whose endpoint is already appointed.
- Active motifs: granted delay (`quranic:root_001520:B002/m01`); duration of time (`quranic:root_001700:B002/m01`); known time (`quranic:root_001671:B001/m01`); temporal delimitation (`quranic:root_001671:B002/m01`); appointed time and place (`quranic:root_001662:B003/m01`); reckoning and recompense (`quranic:root_000504:B002/m01`); guiding marker (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:35-38 (دِّينِ, يَوْمِ, أَنظِرْ, مُنظَرِينَ, وَقْتِ, مَعْلُومِ); 15:43 (مَوْعِدُ).
- Synthesis: Respite is real but bounded. Its temporal extension remains inside a marked appointment that leads toward reckoning rather than away from it.

#### Subchannel B. Seven-Gate Apportionment
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A gathered total reaches a divided entrance and is allocated portion by portion.
- Active motifs: entrance (`quranic:root_000163:B001/m01`); division into doors (`quranic:root_000163:B002/m01`); sevenfold number (`quranic:root_000669:B001/m01`); assigned portion (`quranic:root_000241:B002/m01`); apportionment (`quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`); complete totality (`quranic:root_001315:B003/m01`); gathering (`quranic:root_000259:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:39, 15:43 (أَجْمَعِينَ); 15:44 (أَبْوَٰبٍ, بَابٍ, سَبْعَةُ, جُزْءٌ, مَّقْسُومٌ, كُلِّ).
- Synthesis: Gathering and division are sequential rather than contradictory. The total is first brought to one destination, then the gate system converts it into assigned shares.

### 14. P4 15:45-60 | Protected Reception
- Semantic invariant: Entry becomes safe when an arriving party is received with peace, recognition, and settled placement.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:45-47 (جَنَّٰتٍ, عُيُونٍ, ٱدْخُلُ, سَلَٰمٍ, ءَامِنِينَ, سُرُرٍ, مُّتَقَٰبِلِينَ), 15:51-53 (ضَيْفِ, دَخَلُ, سَلَٰمًا, وَجِلُونَ, نُبَشِّرُ).
- Surprising reach: Garden entry and guest entry are parallel hospitality scenes, but one begins in security while the other must transform fear into good news.

#### Subchannel A. Garden Entry and Settled Facing
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Entrants cross into a sheltered garden, receive safety, and settle face to face beside flowing water.
- Active motifs: enclosed garden (`quranic:root_000266:B003/m01`); flowing spring (`quranic:root_001069:B006/m01`); entry to an interior (`quranic:root_000464:B001/m01`); safety from harm (`quranic:root_000737:B001/m01`); secure trust (`quranic:root_000054:B001/m01`); place of reclining (`quranic:root_000697:B011/m01`); mutual facing (`quranic:root_001198:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:45 (جَنَّٰتٍ, عُيُونٍ); 15:46 (ٱدْخُلُ, سَلَٰمٍ, ءَامِنِينَ); 15:47 (سُرُرٍ, مُّتَقَٰبِلِينَ).
- Synthesis: Garden, spring, entry, safety, and couches form a complete reception scene. Facing one another is the settled social outcome of protected arrival.

#### Subchannel B. Guest Arrival from Fear to News
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Arriving guests enter with a greeting, encounter fear, and replace it with announced joy.
- Active motifs: arrival from place to place (`quranic:root_001464:B001/m01`); incoming report (`quranic:root_001464:B002/m01`); arriving guest (`quranic:root_000924:B002/m01`); entry (`quranic:root_000464:B001/m01`); safety from harm (`quranic:root_000737:B001/m01`); fear felt in the heart (`quranic:root_001629:B001/m01`); news that opens the face with joy (`quranic:root_000120:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:51 (ضَيْفِ, نَبِّئْ); 15:52 (دَخَلُ, سَلَٰمًا, وَجِلُونَ); 15:53 (نُبَشِّرُ, تَوْجَلْ).
- Synthesis: The guest scene is a transition between two interior states. Greeting opens the encounter, fear interrupts it, and good news completes the reception by restoring safety.

### 15. P4 15:45-60 | Interior Release and Protective Covering
- Semantic invariant: Harm is removed from the interior or covered so that its effects no longer bind the person.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:47-50 (نَزَعْ, غِلٍّ, صُدُورِ, يَمَسُّ, نَصَبٌ, غَفُورُ, رَّحِيمُ, عَذَابُ, أَلِيمُ).
- Surprising reach: Rancor behaves like embedded heat or a chain, while forgiveness behaves like a protective covering over exposed injury.

#### Subchannel A. Rancor Extracted from the Chest
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A lodged, heated restraint is uprooted from the chest, ending contact with exhaustion.
- Active motifs: uprooting from a fixed place (`quranic:root_001489:B001/m01`); anatomical chest (`quranic:root_000849:B001/m01`); hidden rancor in the chest (`quranic:root_001102:B005/m01`); heat penetrating the interior (`quranic:root_001102:B002/m01`); restraining collar or chain (`quranic:root_001102:B006/m01`); afflicting contact (`quranic:root_001423:B004/m01`); exhausting hardship (`quranic:root_001507:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:47 (نَزَعْ, غِلٍّ, صُدُورِ); 15:48 (يَمَسُّ, نَصَبٌ).
- Synthesis: The emotional interior is rendered as a body containing heat and restraint. Extraction breaks that attachment, and the cessation of contact prevents fatigue from returning.

#### Subchannel B. Forgiving Cover versus Pain
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Protective covering shields the dependent person from an offense's effects, in contrast to exposed punitive pain.
- Active motifs: protective covering (`quranic:root_001096:B001/m01`); covering an offense and its effects (`quranic:root_001096:B002/m01`); compassionate tenderness (`quranic:root_000552:B001/m01`); obedient dependency (`quranic:root_000973:B003/m01`); painful punishment (`quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`); release and relinquishment (`quranic:root_000737:B012/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:46, 15:52 (سَلَٰمٍ, سَلَٰمًا); 15:49 (عِبَادِ, غَفُورُ, رَّحِيمُ); 15:50 (عَذَابِ, عَذَابُ).
- Synthesis: Forgiveness is not mere cancellation; it covers the person against the continuing trace of injury. The punishment contrast shows the alternative condition in which pain remains uncovered and operative.

### 16. P4 15:45-60 | Impossible Fertility and Restored Hope
- Semantic invariant: Generative possibility crosses an apparent bodily and temporal limit.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:53-56 (غُلَٰمٍ, نُبَشِّرُ, كِبَرُ, مَّسَّ, بَشَّرْ, حَقِّ, قَٰنِطِينَ, رَّحْمَةِ, يَقْنَطُ).
- Surprising reach: Good news opens the face, while mercy reconnects a cut line of hope to the womb and nurture.

#### Subchannel A. Youth Announced to an Aged Body
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: News of youth confronts old age, bodily contact, and the apparent end of generative capacity.
- Active motifs: joy-opening news (`quranic:root_000120:B005/m01`); youthful stage (`quranic:root_001103:B001/m01`); awakened generative desire (`quranic:root_001103:B002/m01`); old age (`quranic:root_001281:B004/m01`); sexual contact (`quranic:root_001423:B002/m01`); disclosed knowledge (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:53 (نُبَشِّرُ, غُلَٰمٍ, عَلِيمٍ); 15:54 (بَشَّرْ, تُبَشِّرُ, كِبَرُ, مَّسَّ); 15:55 (بَشَّرْ).
- Synthesis: The announcement juxtaposes youth, desire, touch, and aged embodiment. Its force lies in reopening a generative scene that the body's chronology appears to have closed.

#### Subchannel B. Womb-Mercy Reconnects Hope
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Compassion and nurture hold a womb-centered possibility against severed hope and lost direction.
- Active motifs: womb (`quranic:root_000552:B003/m01`); compassionate tenderness (`quranic:root_000552:B001/m01`); severance of hope (`quranic:root_001261:B001/m01`); loss of direction (`quranic:root_000913:B001/m01`); fixed truth (`quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`); nurturing completion (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:55 (حَقِّ, قَٰنِطِينَ); 15:56 (رَبِّ, رَّحْمَةِ, ضَّآلُّونَ, يَقْنَطُ).
- Synthesis: Mercy is linked lexically to the womb and functionally to nurture. Despair cuts the line, but fixed truth and continuing care reconnect the promised future.

### 17. P4 15:45-60 | Selective Extraction
- Semantic invariant: A dispatched judgment distinguishes the guilty collective from the kin-group that will be separated and removed.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:57-60 (خَطْبُ, مُرْسَلُونَ, أُرْسِلْ, قَوْمٍ, مُّجْرِمِينَ, مُنَجُّو, أَجْمَعِينَ, ٱمْرَأَتَ, قَدَّرْ, غَٰبِرِينَ).
- Surprising reach: Rescue is an extraction operation, while guilt carries a cutting sense that anticipates the group's severance.

#### Subchannel A. Mission Directed at the Guilty Collective
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Envoys are dispatched on a grave matter toward a community marked by offense and cutting consequence.
- Active motifs: messenger and mission (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`); dispatch (`quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`); grave public matter (`quranic:root_000421:B003/m01`); human collective (`quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`); guilt and offense (`quranic:root_000239:B004/m01`); cutting and severance (`quranic:root_000239:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:57 (خَطْبُ, مُرْسَلُونَ); 15:58 (أُرْسِلْ, قَوْمٍ, مُّجْرِمِينَ).
- Synthesis: Dispatch, grave purpose, collective guilt, and cutting assemble a directed intervention. The mission arrives not at an undifferentiated population but at a group already semantically marked for severance.

#### Subchannel B. Kin Removed, Remainder Left
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A whole household is separated for escape except a woman assigned to remain as residue.
- Active motifs: household line and dependents (`quranic:root_000067:B003/m01`); escape by separation (`quranic:root_001476:B001/m01`); stripping extraction (`quranic:root_001476:B003/m01`); woman or wife (`quranic:root_001409:B001/m01`); planned determination (`quranic:root_001205:B005/m01`); remainder after departure (`quranic:root_001071:B001/m01`); gathered total (`quranic:root_000259:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:59 (ءَالَ, مُنَجُّو, أَجْمَعِينَ); 15:60 (ٱمْرَأَتَ, قَدَّرْ, غَٰبِرِينَ).
- Synthesis: Rescue works by separating and stripping a kin-group out of danger. The decreed exception is described through the opposite operation: not extraction, but being left behind.

### 18. P5 15:61-77 | Mission Arrival and Directed Evacuation
- Semantic invariant: Authoritative arrival converts uncertainty into a precisely routed departure.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:61-65 (جَآءَ, مُرْسَلُونَ, مُّنكَرُونَ, جِئْ, أَتَيْ, حَقِّ, صَٰدِقُونَ, أَسْرِ, قِطْعٍ, يْلِ, ٱتَّبِعْ, أَدْبَٰرَ, يَلْتَفِتْ, ٱمْضُ, تُؤْمَرُ).
- Surprising reach: Truth is not only announced; it becomes route geometry, rear-guard order, and controlled bodily orientation.

#### Subchannel A. Unrecognized Envoys Arrive with Truth
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Unknown arrivals identify their mission by bringing a truthful and fixed matter.
- Active motifs: arrival and occurrence (`quranic:root_000281:B001/m01`); bringing a thing into presence (`quranic:root_000281:B004/m01`); coming (`quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`); fixed truth (`quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`); truthful speech (`quranic:root_000852:B001/m01`); nonrecognition (`quranic:root_001550:B001/m01`); messenger and mission (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:61 (جَآءَ, مُرْسَلُونَ); 15:62 (مُّنكَرُونَ); 15:63 (جِئْ); 15:64 (أَتَيْ, حَقِّ, صَٰدِقُونَ).
- Synthesis: The arrivals move from social uncertainty to mission clarity. Their claim becomes credible through a convergence of coming, bringing, truth, and truthful speech.

#### Subchannel B. Rear-Guarded Night Convoy
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A household crosses a segment of night in ordered file, with a leader behind and no turning aside.
- Active motifs: night travel (`quranic:root_000702:B001/m01`); traversed segment (`quranic:root_001240:B003/m01`); night darkness (`quranic:root_001392:B001/m01`); following behind (`quranic:root_000175:B001/m01`); rear of the group (`quranic:root_000458:B001/m01`); turning toward another direction (`quranic:root_001362:B004/m01`); onward passage (`quranic:root_001430:B001/m01`); binding command (`quranic:root_000051:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:65 (أَسْرِ, قِطْعٍ, يْلِ, ٱتَّبِعْ, أَدْبَٰرَ, يَلْتَفِتْ, ٱمْضُ, تُؤْمَرُ).
- Synthesis: The departure is a tightly controlled movement scene: temporal segment, ordered following, rear position, forbidden turn, and continued passage. Command becomes embodied navigation.

### 19. P5 15:61-77 | Host Boundary and Disoriented Violation
- Semantic invariant: Hospitality establishes a protected social boundary that an intoxicated collective attempts to cross.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:68-72 (ضَيْفِ, تَفْضَحُ, تُخْزُ, ٱتَّقُ, نَنْهَ, بَنَاتِ, فَٰعِلِينَ, سَكْرَتِ, يَعْمَهُ).
- Surprising reach: Shame exposes the host while protection covers the guest; against that boundary, intoxication becomes literal loss of route and judgment.

#### Subchannel A. Guest under the Host's Protection
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A host tries to prevent the exposure and disgrace of guests by invoking prohibition and protective restraint.
- Active motifs: arriving guest (`quranic:root_000924:B002/m01`); exposure of faults (`quranic:root_001161:B001/m01`); disgrace and banishment (`quranic:root_000407:B001/m01`); warding off harm (`quranic:root_001677:B001/m01`); prohibition and restraint (`quranic:root_001560:B001/m01`); household belonging (`quranic:root_000064:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:65, 15:67 (أَهْلِ, أَهْلُ); 15:68 (ضَيْفِ, تَفْضَحُ); 15:69 (تُخْزُ, ٱتَّقُ); 15:70 (نَنْهَ).
- Synthesis: Hospitality is a boundary-maintenance scene. The host's own exposure and shame are at stake because protection makes guest safety part of household responsibility.

#### Subchannel B. Kinship Offered against Intoxicated Wandering
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A lawful kinship route is offered while the opposing crowd acts under intoxicated, directionless fixation.
- Active motifs: marriage entry (`quranic:root_000156:B006/m01`); descent and filiation (`quranic:root_000156:B007/m01`); enacted deed (`quranic:root_001167:B001/m01`); intoxicated loss of clarity (`quranic:root_000725:B001/m01`); bewildered wandering (`quranic:root_001048:B001/m01`); human collective (`quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:62 (قَوْمٌ); 15:71 (بَنَاتِ, فَٰعِلِينَ); 15:72 (سَكْرَتِ, يَعْمَهُ).
- Synthesis: The offered daughters activate the roots' kinship and marriage frame, while the crowd is represented through intoxication and lost direction. The contrast is between a bounded social relation and action without orientation.

### 20. P5 15:61-77 | Verdict Materialized
- Semantic invariant: A settled judgment becomes a physical sequence of cutoff, cry, inversion, and falling stone.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:66 (قَضَيْ, دَابِرَ, مَقْطُوعٌ, مُّصْبِحِينَ), 15:73-74 (صَّيْحَةُ, مُشْرِقِينَ, عَٰلِيَ, سَافِلَ, أَمْطَرْ, حِجَارَةً, سِجِّيلٍ).
- Surprising reach: Legal finality is translated into severed continuity, while rain becomes a poured bucket of stone that reverses vertical order.

#### Subchannel A. Cut Off at Dawn
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A decisive ruling reaches completion as the group's remaining trace is severed by a terrifying cry at daybreak.
- Active motifs: decisive judgment (`quranic:root_001237:B001/m01`); death as executed decree (`quranic:root_001237:B003/m01`); severing (`quranic:root_001240:B001/m01`); eradication of the last trace (`quranic:root_000458:B004/m01`); first light (`quranic:root_000839:B001/m01`); terrifying cry (`quranic:root_000895:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:66 (قَضَيْ, دَابِرَ, مَقْطُوعٌ, مُّصْبِحِينَ); 15:73 (صَّيْحَةُ).
- Synthesis: Judicial and physical closure coincide. The decree cuts the terminal link, and the dawn cry is the event that executes that severance.

#### Subchannel B. Inversion under a Stone Downpour
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The upper is forced below as punitive rain pours hard material from a filled container at sunrise.
- Active motifs: upper side (`quranic:root_001042:B005/m01`); forced descent (`quranic:root_000715:B004/m01`); rainwater (`quranic:root_001431:B001/m01`); punitive rain (`quranic:root_001431:B002/m01`); hard stone (`quranic:root_000296:B003/m01`); filled bucket and outpouring (`quranic:root_000677:B001/m01`); rising eastern light (`quranic:root_000790:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:73 (مُشْرِقِينَ); 15:74 (عَٰلِيَ, سَافِلَ, أَمْطَرْ, حِجَارَةً, سِجِّيلٍ).
- Synthesis: The destruction is a vertical operation: elevation is reversed, then a rain mechanism delivers stone. The bucket sense of *sijill* gives the downpour a concrete pouring vessel.

### 21. P5 15:61-77 | Aftermath Made Legible
- Semantic invariant: Destruction leaves a material trace that can be read in a persistent landscape.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:74-77 (حِجَارَةً, سِجِّيلٍ, ءَايَٰتٍ, مُتَوَسِّمِينَ, سَبِيلٍ, مُّقِيمٍ, ءَايَةً, مُؤْمِنِينَ).
- Surprising reach: Rain brands the ground, stone becomes a marker, and the standing road functions like a record open to discerning readers.

#### Subchannel A. Branded Ground and Recorded Stone
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A punitive fall imprints the terrain, leaving stone and record-like matter as a visible brand.
- Active motifs: visible brand (`quranic:root_001650:B001/m01`); rain that marks soil with growth (`quranic:root_001650:B003/m01`); hard stone (`quranic:root_000296:B003/m01`); written record (`quranic:root_000677:B004/m01`); punitive rain (`quranic:root_001431:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:74 (حِجَارَةً, سِجِّيلٍ, أَمْطَرْ); 15:75 (مُتَوَسِّمِينَ).
- Synthesis: The aftermath is both imprint and inscription. Rain marks the ground, stone fixes the event materially, and *sijill* reframes the deposited matter as a record.

#### Subchannel B. Standing Road Read as a Sign
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A persistent route holds a visible mark that discerning belief can recognize.
- Active motifs: extended road (`quranic:root_000672:B001/m01`); established station (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); visible sign (`quranic:root_000074:B003/m01`); inferential mark (`quranic:root_001650:B002/m01`); settled belief (`quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`); guiding landmark (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:70 (عَٰلَمِينَ); 15:75 (ءَايَٰتٍ, مُتَوَسِّمِينَ); 15:76 (سَبِيلٍ, مُّقِيمٍ); 15:77 (ءَايَةً, مُؤْمِنِينَ).
- Synthesis: The road is not merely nearby; it stands as a durable interpretive surface. Mark, landmark, and belief turn passage through space into recognition of what occurred there.

### 22. P6 15:78-99 | Carved Security and Its Failure
- Semantic invariant: Built enclosure promises safety but cannot withstand a seizure that nullifies accumulated resources.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:78-84 (أَصْحَٰبُ, حِجْرِ, بُيُوتًا, جِبَالِ, يَنْحِتُ, ءَامِنِينَ, أَخَذَتْ, صَّيْحَةُ, مُصْبِحِينَ, أَغْنَىٰ, يَكْسِبُ).
- Surprising reach: The carved house can turn into a grave-house, exposing the difference between architectural enclosure and effective protection.

#### Subchannel A. Mountain Refuge Cut from Stone
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A companion group occupies a stone enclosure, carving dwellings from solid elevation in expectation of security.
- Active motifs: protection through accompaniment (`quranic:root_000844:B002/m01`); hard stone (`quranic:root_000296:B003/m01`); enclosed place (`quranic:root_000296:B004/m01`); solid elevation (`quranic:root_000217:B001/m01`); carving hard material (`quranic:root_001478:B001/m01`); dwelling (`quranic:root_000166:B001/m01`); secure trust (`quranic:root_000054:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:78, 15:80 (أَصْحَٰبُ, حِجْرِ); 15:82 (جِبَالِ, يَنْحِتُ, بُيُوتًا, ءَامِنِينَ).
- Synthesis: Companionship, enclosure, mountain solidity, carving, and dwelling assemble a deliberately fortified habitat. Its safety is engineered into the landscape but remains only an expectation.

#### Subchannel B. Dawn Seizure Nullifies Gain
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A terrifying sound captures the secure settlement at dawn, rendering accumulated gain insufficient as the house becomes a terminal enclosure.
- Active motifs: capture and arrest (`quranic:root_000018:B003/m01`); terrifying cry (`quranic:root_000895:B002/m01`); first light (`quranic:root_000839:B001/m01`); sufficiency (`quranic:root_001110:B002/m01`); acquisition of benefit (`quranic:root_001296:B001/m01`); grave as house (`quranic:root_000166:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:82 (بُيُوتًا); 15:83 (أَخَذَتْ, صَّيْحَةُ, مُصْبِحِينَ); 15:84 (أَغْنَىٰ, يَكْسِبُ).
- Synthesis: Capture, cry, and dawn reverse the settlement's security in a single event. Wealth cannot perform the sufficiency expected of it, and the dwelling's grave sense gives the failed refuge a terminal reframe.

### 23. P6 15:78-99 | Legible Route and Created Interval
- Semantic invariant: A bounded world is made intelligible through routes, marks, disclosure, and a true approaching event.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:79-85 (إِمَامٍ, مُّبِينٍ, مُرْسَلِينَ, ءَايَٰتِ, مُعْرِضِينَ, خَلَقْ, سَّمَٰوَٰتِ, أَرْضَ, بَيْنَ, حَقِّ, ءَاتِيَةٌ).
- Surprising reach: The *imam* is a traversable guide, while the cosmos is read as an interval whose truth is moving toward its audience.

#### Subchannel A. Visible Guide Refused
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A public route and disclosed signs guide the observer, but the addressed group turns its side away from them.
- Active motifs: leader and guide (`quranic:root_000053:B009/m01`); visible disclosure (`quranic:root_000170:B004/m01`); disclosure by speech or sign (`quranic:root_000170:B005/m01`); visible mark (`quranic:root_000074:B003/m01`); turning the side away (`quranic:root_001001:B005/m01`); messenger and message (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:79 (إِمَامٍ, مُّبِينٍ); 15:80 (مُرْسَلِينَ); 15:81 (ءَايَٰتِ, مُعْرِضِينَ).
- Synthesis: Guidance is spatial and semiotic at once: a leading route, a disclosed meaning, and visible marks. Refusal is correspondingly embodied as turning one's side away from a public guide.

#### Subchannel B. True Creation on Approach
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Measured creation spans sky, earth, and the interval between them while a true event approaches.
- Active motifs: measured formation (`quranic:root_000434:B001/m01`); created production (`quranic:root_000434:B002/m01`); overshadowing sky (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`); lower earth (`quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`); interval between sides (`quranic:root_000170:B002/m01`); fixed truth (`quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`); coming and arrival (`quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:85 (خَلَقْ, سَّمَٰوَٰتِ, أَرْضَ, بَيْنَ, حَقِّ, ءَاتِيَةٌ); 15:86 (خَلَّٰقُ).
- Synthesis: Creation is both production and measured span. The approaching event belongs to the same truthful order, so cosmic interval and temporal arrival become two forms of bounded structure.

### 24. P6 15:78-99 | Textual Whole and Partition
- Semantic invariant: Scripture gathers repeated speech into a whole, while hostile handling divides that whole and becomes answerable for the division.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:87 (ءَاتَيْ, سَبْعًا, مَثَانِى, قُرْءَانَ, عَظِيمَ), 15:90-93 (مُقْتَسِمِينَ, قُرْءَانَ, عِضِينَ, نَسْـَٔلَ, أَجْمَعِينَ, يَعْمَلُ).
- Surprising reach: Recitation is an act of gathering; partition is bodily dismemberment, followed by the re-gathering of every actor for questioning.

#### Subchannel A. Gathered and Repeated Recitation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A sevenfold, repeated gift is gathered into recitation and presented as a great whole.
- Active motifs: sevenfold number (`quranic:root_000669:B001/m01`); pairing two together (`quranic:root_000208:B001/m01`); repetition (`quranic:root_000208:B003/m01`); gathering (`quranic:root_001210:B001/m01`); reading and recitation (`quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`); greatness and force (`quranic:root_001029:B001/m01`); grant (`quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:87 (ءَاتَيْ, سَبْعًا, مَثَانِى, قُرْءَانَ, عَظِيمَ).
- Synthesis: Repetition does not fragment the text; it gathers and reinforces it. Number, pairing, recurrence, recitation, and greatness define an integrated textual gift.

#### Subchannel B. Dismembered Text Re-gathered for Account
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A group partitions the recited whole into pieces, after which all actors and deeds are gathered for questioning.
- Active motifs: apportionment (`quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`); reading and recitation (`quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`); dismembered piece (`quranic:root_003789:B002/m01`); transformation into a condition (`quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`); inquiry and demand (`quranic:root_000661:B001/m01`); gathering separated members (`quranic:root_000259:B001/m01`); intentional deed (`quranic:root_001046:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:90 (مُقْتَسِمِينَ); 15:91 (قُرْءَانَ, عِضِينَ, جَعَلُ); 15:92-93 (نَسْـَٔلَ, أَجْمَعِينَ, يَعْمَلُ).
- Synthesis: Partition converts an integrated recitation into body-like pieces. The answering movement reverses that operation by gathering every participant and deed into one field of inquiry.

### 25. P6 15:78-99 | Proclamation under Ridicule
- Semantic invariant: Public truth is forced into the open while hostile speech is turned aside and contained.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:89 (قُلْ, نَّذِيرُ, مُبِينُ), 15:94-97 (ٱصْدَعْ, تُؤْمَرُ, أَعْرِضْ, مُشْرِكِينَ, كَفَيْ, مُسْتَهْزِءِينَ, يَقُولُ).
- Surprising reach: Proclamation is a crack through solid matter, and divine sufficiency acts as a shield against circulating mockery.

#### Subchannel A. Cleaving Declaration
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Commanded speech splits concealment, states the warning aloud, and separates truth from its opposition.
- Active motifs: crack in solid matter (`quranic:root_000850:B001/m01`); public declaration and separation of truth (`quranic:root_000850:B004/m01`); binding command (`quranic:root_000051:B002/m01`); spoken utterance (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); warning that awakens caution (`quranic:root_001488:B001/m01`); disclosure by speech or sign (`quranic:root_000170:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:89 (قُلْ, نَّذِيرُ, مُبِينُ); 15:94 (ٱصْدَعْ, تُؤْمَرُ).
- Synthesis: Declaration is rendered as a material rupture. Command drives speech through resistance, and the resulting split makes the warning public and discriminating.

#### Subchannel B. Turning Aside while Mockery Is Contained
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The speaker turns from association while protective sufficiency contains ridicule and its circulating speech.
- Active motifs: turning the side away (`quranic:root_001001:B005/m01`); theological association (`quranic:root_000791:B002/m01`); sufficiency that completes protection (`quranic:root_001310:B001/m01`); ridicule (`quranic:root_001587:B001/m01`); speech circulating among people (`quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:94 (أَعْرِضْ, مُشْرِكِينَ); 15:95 (كَفَيْ, مُسْتَهْزِءِينَ); 15:97 (يَقُولُ).
- Synthesis: Turning aside is not retreat from proclamation but its paired protective posture. Mockery can circulate socially, yet sufficiency prevents that speech from controlling the declared message.

### 26. P6 15:78-99 | Bodily Discipline under Pressure
- Semantic invariant: Gaze, wing, chest, and posture are disciplined so that external pressure is converted into service.
- Surface relation: direct; 15:88 (تَمُدَّ, عَيْنَيْ, مَتَّعْ, أَزْوَٰجًا, تَحْزَنْ, ٱخْفِضْ, جَنَاحَ, مُؤْمِنِينَ), 15:97-99 (يَقُولُ, يَضِيقُ, صَدْرُ, سَبِّحْ, حَمْدِ, سَّٰجِدِينَ, ٱعْبُدْ, يَقِينُ).
- Surprising reach: Lowering the wing is a social sheltering gesture, while chest constriction is worked through praise, prostration, and sustained service.

#### Subchannel A. Restrained Gaze and Lowered Wing
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The gaze is withheld from distributed enjoyments while the body's wing is lowered toward a believing group.
- Active motifs: longitudinal extension (`quranic:root_001407:B001/m01`); seeing eye (`quranic:root_001069:B001/m01`); pleasurable benefit (`quranic:root_001395:B001/m01`); classified kinds (`quranic:root_000652:B004/m01`); matched peers (`quranic:root_000652:B005/m01`); lowered wing and voice (`quranic:root_000426:B003/m01`); wing as side and shelter (`quranic:root_000263:B002/m01`); grief as inner roughness (`quranic:root_000317:B001/m01`); settled belief (`quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:88 (تَمُدَّ, عَيْنَيْ, مَتَّعْ, أَزْوَٰجًا, تَحْزَنْ, ٱخْفِضْ, جَنَاحَ, مُؤْمِنِينَ).
- Synthesis: The body is directed in two ways: the eye does not extend toward ranked enjoyments, and the wing lowers to shelter companions. Restraint and care are therefore one coordinated posture.

#### Subchannel B. Constricted Chest into Praise and Prostration
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Hostile speech narrows the chest, then praise, lowering, and continuing service reorient the body toward certainty.
- Active motifs: constricted chest (`quranic:root_000925:B002/m01`); anatomical chest (`quranic:root_000849:B001/m01`); spoken utterance (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); worship through glorification (`quranic:root_000666:B001/m01`); praise (`quranic:root_000355:B001/m01`); abasement and lowering (`quranic:root_000675:B001/m01`); obedient service (`quranic:root_000973:B003/m01`); settled certainty (`quranic:root_001696:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:97 (يَقُولُ, يَضِيقُ, صَدْرُ); 15:98 (سَبِّحْ, حَمْدِ, سَّٰجِدِينَ); 15:99 (ٱعْبُدْ, يَقِينُ).
- Synthesis: Speech first registers somatically as narrowed space. Glorification, praise, prostration, and service then form a counter-sequence that converts constriction into durable orientation.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. P1 15:1-15 | Held Water and Timed Release
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water accumulates in a basin and well, is held under shade, and is released through a channel and opening.
- Active motifs: settlement as collected water (`quranic:root_001222:B002/m01`); terminal term as reservoir (`quranic:root_000016:B007/m01`); water channel (`quranic:root_000009:B004/m01`); water issuing through an opening (`quranic:root_001124:B005/m01`); large collected water (`quranic:root_001040:B005/m01`); well (`quranic:root_001248:B007/m01`); shaded water (`quranic:root_000966:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:3-4 (يَعْلَمُ, مَّعْلُومٌ, قَرْيَةٍ); 15:5 (أَجَلَ); 15:7, 15:11 (تَأْتِي, يَأْتِي); 15:12 (قُلُوبِ); 15:14 (فَتَحْ, ظَلُّ).
- Synthesis: The local roots assemble a hydraulic control scene: collection, reservoir, channel, aperture, well, and shaded pool. It usefully reframes the known appointed limit as held capacity whose release occurs through a fixed opening rather than at an arbitrary moment.

### S2. P6 15:78-99 | Beautiful Pardon as Page-Turning
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: At the approach of a true event, pardon turns the exposed side like a page and does so with fitting beauty.
- Active motifs: broad side or surface (`quranic:root_000867:B001/m01`); beautiful turning away from offense (`quranic:root_000867:B002/m01`); turning and inspecting pages (`quranic:root_000867:B003/m01`); beauty in form and action (`quranic:root_000260:B004/m01`); fixed truth (`quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`); coming and arrival (`quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:85 (ٱصْفَحِ, صَّفْحَ, جَمِيلَ, حَقِّ, ءَاتِيَةٌ).
- Synthesis: The same root that names pardon also supplies side and page-turning. The command can therefore be heard as a beautiful turning of the exposed page while truth continues to approach.

### S3. Whole-surah | Cloth Cut into a Coherent Garment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Fine silk is dyed, given its first fold, cut and tailored, patched where torn, and tested for a tight weave, though a patterned finish can misstate the cloth's condition.
- Active motifs: pieces of fine silk (`quranic:root_000700:B003/m01`); cloth saturated with dye (`quranic:root_000722:B009/m01`); first fold of a cloth (`quranic:root_001226:B007/m01`); cutting, tailoring, and sewing a garment (`quranic:root_001240:B012/m01`); new patch in torn cloth (`quranic:root_000850:B009/m01`); well-made tight weave (`quranic:root_000217:B007/m01`); tightly woven cloth (`quranic:root_000347:B010/m01`); patterned dyed cloth that misstates its condition (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:8 (حَقِّ); 15:18 (ٱسْتَرَقَ); 15:22 (أَسْقَيْ); 15:44 (مَّقْسُومٌ); 15:55, 15:64 (حَقِّ); 15:65-66 (قِطْعٍ, مَقْطُوعٌ); 15:80 (كَذَّبَ); 15:82 (جِبَالِ); 15:85 (حَقِّ); 15:90 (مُقْتَسِمِينَ); 15:94 (ٱصْدَعْ).
- Synthesis: The tailoring sequence materializes the surah's tension between textual wholeness and partition. Cutting can serve coherent form when governed by folding, sewing, repair, and tested weave; hostile partition lacks that integrative craft. The truth-root's tight weave supports the recitation's integrity, while cloth that "lies" through its patterned surface usefully pressures ornament as a guarantee of truth.

### S4. Whole-surah | Preparing a Protective Hide
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hairy hide retaining flesh is stripped at the surface, treated with peeled tanning bark and dye leaves, then joined edge to edge.
- Active motifs: removal of the outer skin (`quranic:root_000120:B004/m01`); hide left with hair or wool (`quranic:root_000844:B006/m01`); flesh or fat remaining in a hide (`quranic:root_001102:B012/m01`); acacia leaf or bark used for tanning (`quranic:root_000737:B008/m01`); bark peeled from a tree (`quranic:root_001362:B005/m01`); seaming hide to hide (`quranic:root_000121:B006/m01`); woad leaves used for dye (`quranic:root_001650:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:2 (مُسْلِمِينَ); 15:15 (أَبْصَٰرُ); 15:28, 15:33 (بَشَرًا, بَشَرٍ); 15:46 (سَلَٰمٍ); 15:47 (غِلٍّ); 15:52 (سَلَٰمًا); 15:53-55 (نُبَشِّرُ, بَشَّرْ, تُبَشِّرُ); 15:65 (يَلْتَفِتْ); 15:67 (يَسْتَبْشِرُ); 15:75 (مُتَوَسِّمِينَ); 15:78, 15:80 (أَصْحَٰبُ).
- Synthesis: Hide preparation materializes the surah's extraction-and-covering movement. Protection is produced by exposing residue, stripping a surface, applying treatment, and joining divided edges; it is not mere concealment. This reframes the P4 pairing of interior removal and forgiving cover as transformative craft whose finished shelter depends on what has first been removed.

### S5. Whole-surah | Hunter, Nearing Prey, and Snare
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Hunters set out, prey comes within reach, a set snare entangles it, and the struck animal is checked for death.
- Active motifs: setting out to hunt (`quranic:root_000745:B006/m01`); prey drawing within reach (`quranic:root_000421:B005/m01`); hunter's entangling snare (`quranic:root_000791:B006/m01`); checking whether struck prey has died (`quranic:root_001454:B014/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:14, 15:16, 15:22 (سَّمَآءِ); 15:23 (نُمِيتُ); 15:57 (خَطْبُ); 15:85 (سَّمَٰوَٰتِ); 15:94 (مُشْرِكِينَ).
- Synthesis: The hunting sequence materializes P3's adorned deviation as staged capture: approach becomes the hunter's opportunity, association resonates with entanglement, and death is the checked outcome. It usefully pressures the apparent agency of the deviating subject by exposing the predator-prey asymmetry beneath an attractive route.

### S6. Whole-surah | Lot Arrows, Loss, and Assigned Share
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hide receptacle gathers gaming arrows; named arrows distinguish positions, and the round resolves into fortune, an assigned share, or a losing arrow and forfeited stake.
- Active motifs: hide receptacle gathering lot arrows (`quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`); al-musaffah, the sixth maysir arrow (`quranic:root_000867:B011/m01`); al-mu'alla among the gaming arrows (`quranic:root_001042:B009/m01`); losing lot-arrow and forfeited stake (`quranic:root_000458:B019/m01`); fortune or chance (`quranic:root_000560:B006/m01`); appointed share raised for its recipient (`quranic:root_001507:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:20 (رَٰزِقِينَ); 15:25, 15:28, 15:36, 15:39 (رَبَّ, رَبُّ, رَبِّ); 15:48 (نَصَبٌ); 15:56 (رَبِّ); 15:65-66 (أَدْبَٰرَ, دَابِرَ); 15:74 (عَٰلِيَ); 15:85 (ٱصْفَحِ, صَّفْحَ); 15:86, 15:92, 15:98-99 (رَبَّ, رَبِّ).
- Synthesis: The lot-arrow practice usefully pressures the surah's gate apportionment and textual partition by presenting a rival distribution mechanism. A gaming vessel can make allocation look ordered while named lots, fortune, and forfeiture govern the result; against that contingent partition, the surah's measured decree and accountable recitation assign portions without surrendering them to chance.

## Cross-Pericope Channels

### X1. P1 -> P2 -> P6 | Guarded Speech and Adversarial Access
- Pericopes: P1 15:1-15; P2 15:16-25; P6 15:78-99.
- Bridge: Repeated scene signature and role progression: an ordered utterance, a custody boundary, an adversarial receiver or interceptor, and a protective counteraction.
- Canonical placements: 1A "Assembled Readable Artifact"; 3A "Messenger Speech Recast as Derangement"; 5B "Covert Listener under Pursuit"; 25A "Cleaving Declaration"; 25B "Turning Aside while Mockery Is Contained".
- Synthesis: The adversarial role progresses from an audience that pathologizes delivered speech, through an interceptor who steals a hearing, to mockers confronting public proclamation. Assembly, pursuit, declaration, and protective sufficiency preserve the invariant that hostile access can pressure the message without acquiring custody of it.

### X2. P1 -> P3 -> P5 -> P6 | Appointment Becomes Dawn Verdict
- Pericopes: P1 15:1-15; P3 15:26-44; P5 15:61-77; P6 15:78-99.
- Bridge: Causal sequence and repeated scene signature: assigned limit, bounded respite, terminal dawn, terrifying cry, and completed seizure.
- Canonical placements: 2A "Settlement Entered in the Ledger"; 2B "Neither Overtaking nor Deferring"; 13A "Fixed Respite"; 20A "Cut Off at Dawn"; 22B "Dawn Seizure Nullifies Gain".
- Synthesis: P1 states the rule of a settlement's fixed entry and endpoint, and P3 places even granted delay inside that rule. P5 and P6 then repeat the same execution signature at dawn, converting temporal appointment into cutoff and seizure. The paired events are enacted cases of the earlier invariant, not merely two judgments on a common topic.

### X3. P1 -> P2 -> P5 | Controlled Descent Reversed
- Pericopes: P1 15:1-15; P2 15:16-25; P5 15:61-77.
- Bridge: Repeated scene signature and reversal: held reserve, controlled release, descending cargo, recipient surface, and material outcome.
- Canonical placements: S1 "Held Water and Timed Release"; 6A "Treasury, Keeper, and Meter"; 6B "Wind-Carried Irrigation"; 20B "Inversion under a Stone Downpour"; 21A "Branded Ground and Recorded Stone".
- Synthesis: The hydraulic reserve in P1 and measured rain in P2 establish descent as administered transfer. P5 preserves that mechanism but reverses its cargo and outcome: drinkable water becomes poured stone, and nourished ground becomes branded ground. Control remains invariant while provision turns into verdict.

### X4. P2 -> P3 -> P4 -> P6 | Receptive Substrate Activated
- Pericopes: P2 15:16-25; P3 15:26-44; P4 15:45-60; P6 15:78-99.
- Bridge: Shared invariant, repeated scene signature, and scale progression: receptive substrate, incoming generative force, measured formation, and realized potential.
- Canonical placements: 7A "Soil to Sprout"; 7B "Conception, Milk, and Drink"; 10A "Measured Resonant Body"; 10B "Vital Breath and Penetrating Fire-Wind"; 16A "Youth Announced to an Aged Body"; 16B "Womb-Mercy Reconnects Hope"; 23B "True Creation on Approach".
- Synthesis: The same role structure moves from soil and conception to formed matter, animated bodies, reopened womb-potential, and finally the created cosmic interval. What changes is scale and activating medium; what persists is measured reception becoming life or formed actuality. The progression therefore connects generation, animation, restored fertility, and creation by operation rather than by topic.

### X5. P4 -> P5 | Guests Become Envoys and Evacuation Guides
- Pericopes: P4 15:45-60; P5 15:61-77.
- Bridge: Causal sequence and role progression: welcomed guests disclose an envoy role, announce selective extraction, arrive at the threatened household, receive host protection, and direct escape.
- Canonical placements: 14B "Guest Arrival from Fear to News"; 17A "Mission Directed at the Guilty Collective"; 17B "Kin Removed, Remainder Left"; 18A "Unrecognized Envoys Arrive with Truth"; 18B "Rear-Guarded Night Convoy"; 19A "Guest under the Host's Protection".
- Synthesis: The P4 guests are progressively identified as agents of the P5 evacuation. Their social role reverses twice: guests bearing news become envoys of judgment, then protected dependents in Lot's house become guides who protect and route that house. The bridge is the same participants carrying one mission across the pericope boundary.

### X6. P3 -> P5 -> P6 | Governed Route versus Disoriented Capture
- Pericopes: P3 15:26-44; P5 15:61-77; P6 15:78-99.
- Bridge: Repeated scene signature and contrast: guide or governing line, oriented mover, visible route or mark, rival lure, and divergent destination.
- Canonical placements: 12A "Straight Route under Authority"; 12B "Decorated Track into a Pit"; 19B "Kinship Offered against Intoxicated Wandering"; 21B "Standing Road Read as a Sign"; 23A "Visible Guide Refused"; S5 "Hunter, Nearing Prey, and Snare".
- Synthesis: P3 establishes the contrast between governed straightness and attractive capture. P5 repeats it as a lawful social route opposed by intoxicated wandering, then leaves a standing road as an interpretive mark; P6 presents another public guide that can still be refused. The hunting scene supplies the recurring deviation's role structure: lure and approach culminate in capture rather than free passage.

### X7. P2 -> P3 -> P6 | Gather, Apportion, Re-gather
- Pericopes: P2 15:16-25; P3 15:26-44; P6 15:78-99; whole-surah lexical materializations S3 and S6.
- Bridge: Repeated scene signature, contrast, and reversal: a whole is assembled, division is applied, order is either preserved or violated, and separated members are gathered for destination or account.
- Canonical placements: 9B "Fore and Aft Driven to One Destination"; 13B "Seven-Gate Apportionment"; 24A "Gathered and Repeated Recitation"; 24B "Dismembered Text Re-gathered for Account"; S3 "Cloth Cut into a Coherent Garment"; S6 "Lot Arrows, Loss, and Assigned Share".
- Synthesis: P2 gathers separated temporal ranks, P3 apportions a gathered total, and P6 opposes integrated recitation to hostile partition before gathering actors for account. Tailoring clarifies that governed cutting can preserve a whole's intended form, while lot arrows pressure partition with the possibility of contingent allocation. The decisive invariant is not indivisibility but whether division remains answerable to the order that gathered the whole.

### X8. P1 -> P4 -> P6 | Interior Receives, Releases, and Reorients
- Pericopes: P1 15:1-15; P4 15:45-60; P6 15:78-99; whole-surah lexical materialization S4.
- Bridge: Role progression and repeated scene signature: bounded interior, inserted or lodged content, extraction or covering, pressure, and transformed orientation.
- Canonical placements: 1B "Delivered, Retained, and Threaded"; 15A "Rancor Extracted from the Chest"; 15B "Forgiving Cover versus Pain"; 26B "Constricted Chest into Praise and Prostration"; S4 "Preparing a Protective Hide".
- Synthesis: P1 makes the interior a receptive site into which the message is threaded. P4 changes the operation from insertion to removal and protective covering, while P6 shows hostile speech constricting the chest before praise and prostration reorient it. Hide preparation materializes the shared mechanism: effective covering follows exposure, removal, treatment, and joining rather than untouched concealment.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S7. Whole-surah | Executing a Right-Deed at the Scribe's Desk
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A scribe prepares a deed preserving a right, draws ink into a pen nib, and adds a concluding notation whose trace remains on the document.
- Active motifs: deed preserving a right (`quranic:root_000516:B008/m01`); pen nib (`quranic:root_000750:B009/m01`); inkpot supplying ink to a pen (`quranic:root_001407:B005/m01`); writer's concluding notation and written trace (`quranic:root_001675:B011/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:6, 15:9 (ذِّكْرُ, ذِّكْرَ); 15:13 (سُنَّةُ); 15:19 (مَدَدْ); 15:26, 15:28 (مَّسْنُونٍ); 15:29 (قَعُ); 15:33 (مَّسْنُونٍ); 15:88 (تَمُدَّ).
- Synthesis: Nib, inkpot, deed, and closing notation form a complete document-execution process. This materializes P1's assembled readable artifact as an operative record of right and supports P6's account scene: inscription can preserve a claim in a form that remains available for later inquiry.

### S8. Whole-surah | Raised Song and Rhythmic Handclapping
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A singer projects a raised, modulated voice; listeners receive it as pleasurable song while hands clap in rhythm.
- Active motifs: projected audible voice (`quranic:root_000239:B008/m01`); pleasurable listening and song (`quranic:root_000741:B007/m01`); singing voice and modulated reading (`quranic:root_001110:B003/m01`); raised rider's chant (`quranic:root_001507:B009/m01`); handclapping (`quranic:root_000867:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:12, 15:58 (مُجْرِمِينَ, مُّجْرِمِينَ); 15:18 (سَّمْعَ); 15:48 (نَصَبٌ); 15:84 (أَغْنَىٰ); 15:85 (ٱصْفَحِ, صَّفْحَ).
- Synthesis: Voiced projection, song, pleasurable audition, raised chant, and clapping complete a participatory performance scene. It usefully pressures P1 and P6's proclamation scenes by separating acoustic involvement from truthful reception: an utterance can delight and coordinate listeners without its disclosed claim being accepted.

### S9. Whole-surah | Sharpening the Woodcarver's Adze
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A woodcarver hones an iron edge on a whetstone, works the timber with an adze, and leaves shavings beneath the cut.
- Active motifs: sharpening and polishing iron on a whetstone (`quranic:root_000750:B003/m01`); adze used to carve wood (`quranic:root_001207:B008/m01`); shavings fallen from carving (`quranic:root_001478:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:13 (سُنَّةُ); 15:24 (مُسْتَقْدِمِينَ); 15:26, 15:28, 15:33 (مَّسْنُونٍ); 15:82 (يَنْحِتُ).
- Synthesis: Whetstone, adze, timber, and fallen shavings form a maintained subtractive craft. The wood medium does not duplicate P6's mountain material; instead, the parallel operation materializes carving as edge-dependent labor and usefully pressures engineered permanence by exposing the removal and residue on which a carved refuge depends.

### S10. Whole-surah | Date Grove, Harvest Basket, and Gleanings
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A pit-grown palm stands beside a named sweet cultivar; fresh dates enter a woven palm-leaf basket, while fallen fruit and pits are gleaned after cutting.
- Active motifs: unnamed pit-grown date palm (`quranic:root_000259:B011/m01`); hard, sweet black date cultivar (`quranic:root_000895:B005/m01`); woven palm-leaf basket for fresh dates (`quranic:root_000464:B010/m01`); post-harvest gleanings, dried dates, and pits (`quranic:root_000239:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:12, 15:58 (مُجْرِمِينَ, مُّجْرِمِينَ); 15:30, 15:39, 15:43, 15:59, 15:92 (أَجْمَعُونَ, أَجْمَعِينَ); 15:46, 15:52 (ٱدْخُلُ, دَخَلُ); 15:73, 15:83 (صَّيْحَةُ).
- Synthesis: Cultivar, basket, fresh yield, and post-cutting residue form a compact date-harvest economy. This extends P2's soil-to-sprout process to a differentiated endpoint: measured provision becomes particular fruit gathered for use, while the remaining dates and pits make the harvest boundary materially visible.

### S11. Whole-surah | Hive, Swarm Leader, and Honey Extraction
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A bee swarm occupies a hive, follows its leading bee, and yields honey that is removed with a collector's tool and thickens after extraction.
- Active motifs: beehive (`quranic:root_000436:B012/m01`); swarm of bees or wasps (`quranic:root_000458:B014/m01`); leading bee followed by the swarm (`quranic:root_001444:B008/m01`); honey collector's extracting tool (`quranic:root_001489:B010/m01`); honey thickening at its final stage (`quranic:root_000067:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:7-8, 15:28, 15:30 (مَلَٰٓئِكَةِ, مَلَٰٓئِكَةَ, مَلَٰٓئِكَةُ); 15:10, 15:13 (أَوَّلِينَ); 15:13 (خَلَتْ); 15:47 (نَزَعْ); 15:59, 15:61 (ءَالَ); 15:65-66 (أَدْبَٰرَ, دَابِرَ).
- Synthesis: Hive, organized swarm, leader, extraction tool, and thickened product form a complete apiary scene. It materializes P2's stored-provision mechanism as collective production under guidance: a bounded reserve is not indiscriminately open but becomes available through directed removal and a final change of state.

### S12. Whole-surah | Ritual Visit to an Erected Slaughter Stone
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A worshipper intentionally visits an erected cult stone, treats it as an object of service, slaughters upon it, and pours sacrificial blood over it.
- Active motifs: worship and object made worthy of worship (`quranic:root_000047:B001/m01`); intentional ritual visit (`quranic:root_001044:B004/m01`); erected cult stone (`quranic:root_001507:B002/m01`); slaughter and poured blood at the stone (`quranic:root_001507:B002/m02`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:48 (نَصَبٌ); 15:69 (ٱللَّهَ); 15:72 (عَمْرُ); 15:96 (ٱللَّهِ, إِلَٰهًا).
- Synthesis: Approach, erected object, worship, slaughter, and blood form one concrete rite. It usefully pressures P3's bodily alignment and P6's command to worship by showing that intensity and procedural completeness do not establish right orientation; the authority and object governing the act remain decisive.

### S13. Whole-surah | Pricing and Settling a Deferred Sale
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Goods are appraised, coin is checked for equal weight, a deferred sale leaves a debt and enforceable right, and present cash settles the claim.
- Active motifs: appraisal and pricing of goods (`quranic:root_001273:B010/m01`); balance for equitable weight and accounting (`quranic:root_001645:B002/m01`); dinar of equal standard weight (`quranic:root_001273:B015/m01`); financial debt and deferred sale (`quranic:root_000504:B003/m01`); present cash or coin opposed to debt (`quranic:root_001069:B011/m01`); proprietary right held as a claim (`quranic:root_000347:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 15:8, 15:55, 15:64, 15:85 (حَقِّ); 15:15, 15:41, 15:58, 15:62, 15:76 (قَوْمٌ, مُسْتَقِيمٌ, قَوْمٍ, مُّقِيمٍ); 15:19 (مَّوْزُونٍ); 15:35 (دِّينِ); 15:45, 15:88 (عُيُونٍ, عَيْنَيْ).
- Synthesis: Appraisal, balance, standard coin, debt, cash, and claim form one deferred-sale settlement. This materializes P2's measured provision as an accountable social exchange and supports P6's inquiry: measure is not only quantity but the condition by which delayed obligations can be identified, claimed, and discharged.
